Kyler Murray out for Monday vs. Bills, week-to-week with hamstring injury

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Kyler Murray’s hamstring injury is serious enough that it will sideline him for at least a week.

Vikings head coach Kevin O’Connell told reporters in his Thursday press conference that Murray is out for Monday night’s game against the Bills with a hamstring strain and is considered week-to-week.

Carson Wentz will start Week 9, which will be his second start of the season.

“For [Murray], his movement skills, and the way he plays the game, we do feel like we’d be putting him at risk of possibly turning this into a more significant injury than it is by trying to have him work through it here early on,” O’Connell said. “So, Carson will start the football game and Max [Brosmer] will back him up.”

While it didn’t seem like O’Connell was too concerned about Murray’s availability earlier in the week, further testing revealed the extent of the issue.

“I think hamstrings are always unique,” O’Connell said. "[Y]ou always have to understand that whatever that picture or that imaging says, sometimes it’s an injury you can really hone in on. That’s why I wanted to be certain before I went speculating on Monday just because I wanted to be sure it wasn’t something that would prohibit him from playing — until it was.

“I’m not going to get into the details of the grade or the injury or the level medically speaking, but just knowing that Kyler needs his mobility — it’s a big, huge part of what makes him an effective player. If that puts him at risk of potentially adding to the grade of the injury, we can’t do that. It’s a long season. We hope to get him back and get him going.”

O’Connell noted injured reserve is not a consideration for Murray with this injury.

Wentz stepped in and played after Murray suffered a concussion earlier this season. Wentz completed 23-of-39 passes against the Packers and Bears for 276 yards with three touchdowns and no interceptions.

“Obviously, there’s a ton of confidence in Carson,” O’Connell said. “He’s had a major, major role in two wins this year against two really good teams. He’s a steadying presence and a guy we know can go in and play good football, that’s why we feel so great about having him here.

“He’s going to throw a lot of completions. He’s going to put the ball in play. He’s got such a high, high understanding of not only the offense but what he’s looking at on the other side. He has 100 games of experience. He’s the perfect guy in a moment where you can’t have Kyler out there at full strength to go in there. And we feel very strongly about when Carson’s in there, we’re going to get really effective quarterback play.”

Minnesota’s offense has struggled to generate yards and points this season, with the club ranking No. 27 in scoring and No. 29 in total yards. We’ll see if Wentz can provide a spark to change that on Monday night.

Roma vs Sassuolo - prediction, team news, lineups

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Jonathan O'Shea | 05 Nov 2026 18:38

Following their latest midweek meltdown, Roma must try to get back on track when they welcome Sassuolo to Stadio Olimpico on Saturday evening.

After another Champions League implosion, the Giallorossi will seek an immediate response, as Serie A's best attack hosts its worst defence.

Three weeks on from throwing away a golden chance to beat mighty Real Madrid, it was a similar story for Roma against more illustrious opposition on Tuesday night.

This time, the Giallorossi wasted a two-goal lead at Old Trafford, where they eventually crumbled and conceded three times after the break, losing 3-2 to crisis club Manchester United.

As a result, Roma are only inside the Champions League playoff zone by virtue of goal difference, and they face a fight to avoid elimination at the league phase.

While their return to UEFA's elite level may not be going to plan, they remain one of only four teams still unbeaten across Europe's top five leagues, alongside city rivals Lazio, and Bundesliga giants Bayern Munich and Borussia Dortmund.

The capital club recently followed a thrilling 3-3 Derby del Sole draw against Napoli with 4-2 and 5-2 wins over Cagliari and Udinese respectively, leaving them third in Serie A - albeit four points behind shock leaders Lazio.

Though, Roma have started to leak goals - 10 in their last four games across all competitions - they possess the most lethal attack in Italy's top flight.

A tally of 28 goals from their first 10 league matches is a new club record, and the last side to start at that rate was Atalanta in 2019, when Gian Piero Gasperini was their head coach.

Now, after a chastening trip to England, Gasperini will aim to extend Roma's head-to-head dominance over Sassuolo and head into the international break on a high.

Despite their status as Serie A's provincial upstarts, Sassuolo have only posted two wins from 24 league meetings with Roma, losing all of the last four.

During that time, their sole success at the Olimpico was a 4-3 victory in March 2023, when Armand Lauriente scored twice and set up one more.

The French winger also bagged a brace in Monday's chaotic 4-2 reverse to Fiorentina, as Sassuolo's fourth straight defeat dropped them down to 17th place.

Having previously lost 6-2 to Lazio - a few days after conceding four to Frosinone - the Neroverdi now have Serie A's worst defensive record.

The Emilian club have already leaked 26 goals, and that is their highest tally at this stage of any top-flight campaign - including their relegation season of 2024-25.

So, with their last away win dating back to February, it seems a tall task for Alberto Aquilani to defeat his old club in Rome.

Although they endured a nightmare at the 'Theatre of Dreams', Roma can at least take some solace from the return to training of Manu Kone and Paulo Dybala.

It remains to be seen if either will be risked this weekend, when Lorenzo Pellegrini and Mile Svilar will still be sidelined.

Svilar's deputy Pier Luigi Gollini is also a doubt after missing the trip to Manchester with a back injury, while Dutch wing-back Devyne Rensch has just been ruled out for the next three weeks.

Donyell Malen sits joint-second in the Capocannoniere rankings with eight Serie A goals, and he is the leading scorer in Europe's top leagues for 2026, with 22.

Sassuolo strikers Kieron Bowie and Sebastiano Esposito have been far less potent, and only midfielder Vasilije Adzic has scored more than two goals for the Neroverdi this term.

Lauriente did find the net twice on Monday, though, and he has previously been involved in four league goals against Roma.

While Kristian Thorstvedt can return from suspension, the visitors will head south without Fedde Leysen, Ismael Kone and Daniel Boloca, plus ex-Roma winger Cristian Volpato.

Vasquez; Mancini, Ndicka, Hermoso; Molina, Cristante, Pisilli, Wesley; Soule, Mora; Malen

Turati; Cinquegrano, Cande, Idzes, Obrador; Adzic, Matic, Thorstvedt; Berardi, Esposito, Lauriente

Fired up by another Champions League mishap, free-scoring Roma should resume normal service on home turf.

So, ailing Sassuolo - who have taken just one point from their last seven away fixtures - will suffer a fifth straight defeat in Serie A.

Cifuentes hails Timbers’ togetherness as Decision Day looms

📅 Nov 05, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Syndicated reporting provided by Stats Perform and reviewed by the SoccerNews editorial team.

Marti Cifuentes has hailed his Portland Timbers players for sticking together all season, as they prepare for their MLS Cup Playoff destiny to be decided against Austin FC.

Portland go to Texas for their final game of the regular season on Saturday, sitting eighth in the Western Conference.

The Timbers could still overtake the LA Galaxy and claim an automatic playoff spot, but there is also a chance they could fall out of the wild-card spots – the Colorado Rapids, Seattle Sounders, Real Salt Lake and Minnesota United are all within two points of them.

A victory over Austin – who are 14th in the West and eliminated from playoff contention – would ensure Portland make the postseason regardless of results elsewhere.

But whatever happens, Cifuentes is proud of the way his players have taken to his methods since he took charge in July.

"I think there is always a relationship between confidence and getting results. Usually, the way it works is the better you play, the results will come," he said ahead of the game.

"Then when you're winning, the confidence builds. The credit that I want to give to all those guys is that, during this run, when things were not going our way, the team was growing week by week.

"The guys kept working at exactly the same level, with exactly the same belief. They bought into what we wanted to implement.

"So, it's not just a coach coming here saying, 'We're going to do this', and that's all.

"We've been adjusting certain things during this process, but they are in a good moment now.

"In football and all sports, what you did yesterday is gone. You just have to push on and get the next one."

While Portland registered a crucial 3-1 victory over the Houston Dynamo last time out, Austin went down 4-1 to the Galaxy in their most recent match, with Zan Kolmanic being sent off late on.

Building momentum  All focus shifts to Decision Day in ATX  https://t.co/oTPGfGYN6l

— Portland Timbers (@TimbersFC) November 3, 2026

Austin have conceded 63 goals this season, their most in a single season in club history. At the other end, they have only netted 42 times, with their joint-leading scorers Ramirez and Murto Uzuni scoring nine apiece.

Ramirez will be particularly keen to finish in double figures, having not done so in MLS since his first season with Minnesota United in 2017.

Portland scored twice in the final half-hour against Houston last time out, with Velde netting after the 60-minute mark for the fourth time this year.

Overall, his 11 goals are the most by any Timbers player this campaign, ahead of Kevin Kelsy on nine.

The Portland Timbers have a narrow lead in their all-time series against Austin, with six wins to four (D2).

However, they have only managed one win in the last four encounters between the sides (D1 L2), after going six without losing (W5 D1).

The Timbers have also not lost in Austin since 2021, though only a victory will assure them of a place in the postseason without relying on favours from elsewhere.

They should fancy their chances against Austin, who have only won one of their last eight matches (D2 L5).

Despite that run, Austin have not been held scoreless since September 19, against FC Dallas, finding the net in seven straight games – that could mean there is some Decision-Day drama in store.
